# IvritSuite Improvement Log

The memory of the continuous-improvement loop. **Read this first every session.** One concern per
iteration, one commit per iteration. Prioritization: P1 (data loss/security/broken core/export
corruption) > P2 (silently wrong output/undo holes/a11y blockers) > P3 (perf/dead UI/confusing
copy/consistency) > P4 (polish). Tie-breakers: (1) affects teachers' saved work, (2) affects the
printed/exported artifact a student receives, (3) dual-audience (Hebrew + secular) wins, (4) smallest diff.

## Candidates (prioritized, top = next)

_(**Refreshed S166.** The maintainer asked to clear the backlog, and **every open code candidate the
S165 list carried was closed this session** — the 7 Font Maker sliders, both Font Maker P4s, and the
generator transliteration residual. What remains below is: the **new Pass-K finding** (Font Maker's
auto-detect modal is entirely hardcoded English despite complete authored+translated keys — **top
candidate**, pure wiring, 0 new copy), an **orphaned-key census** whose detector needs work, one
standing gate-2 decision the maintainer already deferred (**do NOT re-ask**), and one
informational torah print residual with no near-term fix. The **feature seed queue still has one
item**, un-built for a sixth session — offer it at gate 1 FIRST.)_

- [x] ✅ DONE 2026-07-29 (out-of-band, user-directed), adb257d + d7ca797 | Hebrew_Font_Maker.html | **The auto-detect modal spoke English in a Hebrew UI, and 8 more scattered sites with it — all pure wiring, 0 new keys.** The complete 14-key `fontmaker.ad.*` family had been authored, translated and never referenced (`grep -c` → 0). Wired via a `_adT(key, fallback, params)` helper (these panels re-render without `applyStaticI18n`), which **falls back to English rather than returning the key** — so it cannot reproduce the pre-ready-i18n defect. The 8 siblings (trace-first-jump, autodetect title, kern Delete, re-seed side columns, 3 export toasts, no-compression) likewise reuse their existing keys with English fallback arms. **Two findings the candidate did not predict:** (1) the "one genuinely new key" turned out to be unnecessary — the nested `<svg role="group">` aria-label was redundant because its host `#adStage` already carries a localized, strictly-more-useful name, so the nested English name was **removed** rather than translated; (2) **two duplicate keys exist** — `fontmaker.adpick.box_title` restates `ad.pick_title` and `ad.pick_group_aria` restates the already-wired `fontmaker.aria.assign_box`. The picker title now uses the family key so the heading agrees with its own buttons (a **1-word shipped-Hebrew change**, הקצו vs שייכו — both maintainer-authored; flagged, not hidden). Both dead keys were **left in the CSV and logged rather than deleted** — deletion is the maintainer's call. Pure i18n → no `FONT_MAKER_VERSION` bump. Verified EN/HE × light/dark × 1280/800: both summary branches, picker panel, stage, 0 raw keys, 0 pageerrors; the 9 reused keys resolve with parameters and the no-compression toast was fired through the real `status()` path and read back in Hebrew. **Two vacuous assertions were caught and fixed mid-verification** — the SVG check passed only because the stage had never been rendered, and the live-status check queried the wrong element id, returned `""` and still passed. Both now fail if untrue.

- [x] 🚫 WON'T-FIX (2026-07-29, maintainer-accepted recommendation to skip) | locales/ui-strings.csv + all pages | **An orphaned-key census was attempted and abandoned as untrustworthy.** Three detectors gave 721 / 2 / 424 because dynamically-built keys (`I18n.t('prefix' + slug)`) resist regex. The *useful* output — the per-family triage — was obtained anyway: the `fontmaker.ad.*` family was the real find (now fixed), `shared.footer.*` is orphaned **by design** (footer attribution stays English per the CLAUDE.md scope boundary), and the `*.glyphname.*` families are the standing gate-2 item. A trustworthy count would need a real `I18n.t()` call-site resolver; the number buys nothing the triage did not. **Newly known dead keys** (from the auto-detect work, left in place for a maintainer delete): `fontmaker.adpick.box_title`, `fontmaker.ad.pick_group_aria`.

- [x] ✅ DONE S166, 3219909 | Hebrew_Font_Maker.html | **7 sliders still have no accessible name** (S164 — the deliberate residual of 18067f0, which fixed 13 of 20). These are the ones whose visible label is **not** on the slider's own line, so each needs a per-control decision rather than a mechanical rewrite: L8768 (`<div class="inline">Width` — **hardcoded English**, so it needs an i18n key too, not just a name), L10334/10338/10340/10343 (labels rendered elsewhere in the block), L10463 `#tropSpread` and L10484 `#vowelPairDx` (both have ids, so `aria-labelledby` pointing at the existing visible label may be cleaner than `aria-label`). Detection: static scan for `<input type="range"[^>]*>` lacking `aria-label`/`aria-labelledby` — **do not audit rendered sliders only**, the owning panels need specific state to render and a headless-only sweep under-counts 20 as 1. Font-maker was at its 2-iteration cap when this was found. | found: 2026-07-27, S164

- [x] ✅ DONE S166, 6975026 | Hebrew_Font_Maker.html | **The welcome modal and the "Continue where you left off?" prompt can stack.** **FIXED S166:** `maybeRestoreAutosave()` now defers while the welcome card, the mobile warning, or the tour is up, and is re-offered from `dismissWelcome`/`tourEnd` — reusing the deferral `maybeShowWelcome()` already had for the mobile warning, so the file gained a pattern rather than a special case. The probe found **one more case than the ledger recorded**: the prompt could also land on top of the guided tour (the welcome card's own "take the tour" path), so the tour is guarded too. Verified with an authentically seeded IndexedDB autosave (gzip record, id `'current'`) — pre-fix both dialogs open together AND the prompt covers the tour; post-fix welcome shows alone then the prompt follows, the tour path waits for `tourEnd`, a returning `welcomeSeen` user is unchanged; 0 pageerrors across 12 runs (light/dark × 1280/800). _Original entry:_ Both were open simultaneously in a probe where `localStorage` was empty (so `hebrewBlender_welcomeSeen` was unset) but IndexedDB still held an autosave — two dialogs, four buttons, at once. Requires partially-cleared storage, so a real user hits it rarely (browser "clear cookies and site data" usually clears both). Cheap fix if ever wanted: have `maybeRestoreAutosave()` wait on the welcome modal being dismissed. Found S163 Pass H.

- [x] ✅ DONE S167, e5bca4e | hebrew_blend_generator.html | **Dagesh Tav toggle silently wipes the real-words letter selection VISUALS while the filter stays active.** **FIXED S167 (e5bca4e)** — took the "make the builder self-apply" option rather than mirroring the line, so all three callers are covered at once: `initRwLetterGrid` now re-derives `.selected` from `rwLetterFilter`, placed **before `bindTileA11y`**, which seeds `aria-pressed` from the class. That detail turned out to matter — pre-fix the rebuilt tiles also announced `aria-pressed="false"` for letters that were still filtering the pool, so this was an a11y state lie as well as a visual one. Verified 20/20 across light+dark × 1280/800 (select 2 → dagesh on → off → he → en, asserting `.selected` **and** `aria-pressed` both equal the live filter); **negative control on the stashed pre-fix file fails exactly 8 of those checks and passes the language-switch ones** — the precise shape the candidate described. `setDageshTav` rebuilds `#rwLetterGrid` (`innerHTML=''; initRwLetterGrid()`), and the builder applies no selection classes — but `rwLetterFilter` is untouched, so the pool filter keeps applying while every tile looks unselected. Reproduced live: select 2 tiles → toggle dagesh → Set still `ג,ז`, selected classes empty. Silently-wrong UI state, not data loss. Fix shape already exists in the same file: the applyI18n language-switch rebuild re-applies classes from `rwLetterFilter` after `initRwLetterGrid()` (added 2026-07-29) — mirror that line in `setDageshTav`, or make the builder self-apply like `initLockGrids` does. | found: 2026-07-29, audit

- [x] ✅ DONE 2026-07-29 (out-of-band — **maintainer reversed the S157 deferral in-session**), 2241bf8 | hebrew_blend_generator.html | **Letter and vowel names now follow the UI language.** Authored+translated keys that were never referenced, so a teacher working in Hebrew still saw "Alef, Bet, Chaf" and "Aqua, Red, Grey". **Corrected count: 66, not 75** (42 `glyphname` + 24 `vowels.*`; the entry's "24 colour keys" was really 8 colour + 9 sub + 7 sound). **Approach:** slugify the EXISTING English name in the data tables into its key, **falling back to that same English name on a miss** — an unmapped entry degrades to today's behaviour, never a raw key, and the helper is idempotent so a partly-localized list passes through safely. **Coverage measured BEFORE coding** (29/29 letter names, 13/13 colour-picker names slugify cleanly; the 9 sub-labels do NOT — "AH sound" is keyed `ah` — so they get an explicit map). Six surfaces: letter grid, real-words grid, 3 lock grids, vowel chips + sub-labels, group headers, colour-picker rows (visible label + `aria-label` share one source, so both fixed at once). **Two details deliberately preserved:** the cramped tiles build the sofit label with a **non-breaking space** so it cannot wrap mid-name (kept unbreakable in every language — and the screenshot confirms `sofit_suffix` fits, so **no abbreviated key was needed**, closing the one open sub-decision); and the English colour label carries a teaching mnemonic (`<u>A</u>qua` = "A for AH"), kept verbatim when the translation IS the English word. **The bug this nearly shipped:** `applyI18n` had to rebuild three builders on a language switch, and **`initLetters` APPENDS without clearing** (its two existing callers clear first) — so the first version DOUBLED the grid to 62 tiles, half English. **My own assertion hid it**: it checked *whether* Hebrew appeared, not that the count was unchanged and that EVERY name was Hebrew. Tightened to assert count stability + all-Hebrew, which then caught it. **Print boundary tested, not assumed:** a generated worksheet in blend and Name-the-Letter modes with the answer key on contains none of these names (Name-the-Letter prints `combined`, the sound). Verified EN/HE × light/dark × 1280/800 + live switch: 31/31 Hebrew, selection preserved, 0 raw keys, 0 pageerrors.

- [x] ✅ DONE S156, 9e95399 | hebrew_blend_generator.html | **Changing the transliteration style did nothing to a Real Words worksheet** (S155 Pass F dead-guard sweep; S155's naive fix deleted the answer key and was reverted). **FIXED S156** — it was **two** defects: (a) *wrong renderer* — the dead `renderCustomWordlist`/`renderRealWords` guards fell through to a `blends.length` check that is 0 in Real Words mode, so nothing re-rendered; (b) *stale cache* — `rwRenderCache` stores shallow **copies** (`words.map(w => ({...w}))`) that detach from `REAL_WORDS`, so even a correct re-render replayed the old `translitDisplay`. The right target already existed: `onQrAnswerKeyToggle`'s three-branch deterministic re-render (Real Words → `generateRealWords(reuse=true)`, which is why S155's `renderWorksheet(false)` was wrong — that only knows `blends[]`). Extracted it as **`rerenderCurrentSheet()`**, pointed both callers at it, and taught `rebuildTranslitDisplay` to refresh the cached copies (skipping blend-derived items, whose `translit` already holds a rendered string). **Verified:** 30/30 translit cells restyled, answer key kept (2→2 sheets), same words, × light/dark × 1280/800px; blends control stable; QR toggle unregressed.
- [x] ✅ DONE S166, fd71875 | hebrew_blend_generator.html | **Transliteration restyle still won't reach the matching, word-search and crossword drills** (S156, the deliberate residual of 9e95399). Those three cache **derived structures** rather than word copies — `rwRenderCache.versionsItems` (nested per-version arrays), `.snap` (word-search grid + placed words) and `.d` (crossword layout) — so the `items`/`words` refresh added in 9e95399 doesn't reach them, and a style change replays their baked strings. Not silently claimed as fixed. Lower value than the P2 was: these three drills show transliteration far less often (word-search/crossword print Hebrew grids; matching targets are selectable). Fix needs a per-shape refresh (or storing word references instead of derived strings) with care around the grid snapshots. | found: 2026-07-24, S156
 `rerenderAfterTranslitChange()` tries `renderCustomWordlist` then `renderRealWords` behind `typeof … === 'function'` guards; **both functions were folded into `renderWorksheet` long ago and no longer exist**, so both guards are permanently false. The fall-through is gated on `blends.length`, which is **0 in Real Words mode** (its words never populate `blends`). Net effect: a teacher switches the style dropdown to SBL/Brill/etc. and the on-screen *and printed* Real Words sheet keeps the previous style, with no error. **Empirically confirmed** (DOM-sentinel test): Real Words → sentinel survives = no re-render; standard blends (`blends.length` 40) → re-renders correctly. **ATTEMPTED + REVERTED S155:** replacing the guards with `if (#worksheet .sheet && renderWorksheet) renderWorksheet(false)` does fix the re-render — but **`renderWorksheet(false)` called on a Real Words sheet drops the `.sheet.answer-key-sheet` page** (2 sheets → 1, verified by calling `renderWorksheet` **directly**, i.e. independent of the edit). Real Words places transliteration on a separate **answer-key page** by default (`rwTranslitPage`/`rwTrPlace = 'key'`), and a bare re-render doesn't reproduce it — so the naive fix trades a no-op for silently deleting the teacher's answer key, which is strictly worse. **The real fix must route through whatever the Generate path uses to build the answer-key page** (not bare `renderWorksheet`), or `renderWorksheet` must learn to rebuild it — that's an answer-key-pipeline change, beyond a one-concern minimal diff. | found: 2026-07-24, S155 Pass F

- [x] ✅ DONE (maintainer design-call, c1591a8, 2026-07-22) | Hebrew_Font_Maker.html | **Interactive editing stages given an accessible name** (S125 Pass C). **RESOLVED — a guarded `aria-label` naming the current glyph (`labelStage`) on `#stage`/`#meStage`/`#adStage`; NO role change (the `#nodeBar` keyboard path stays authoritative, so `role="application"`/`"img"` deliberately avoided); refreshed from `applyI18n` (renderStage isn't re-run on a live switch) with an English fallback on a pre-locale key-return. Verified EN+HE headless.** _Original analysis:_ The glyph-edit `#stage` / `#meStage` (SVG) and `#adStage` (div) are pointer-driven editing canvases with no `role`/`aria-label`/`tabindex` — an SR user gets no context for what they are. Nuanced: they're editing surfaces, not images, and the *keyboard* path is the separate `#nodeBar` toolbar (roving tabindex, already accessible), so `role="application"` risks trapping SR users and `role="img"` mislabels an interactive surface. A safe slice would be an `aria-label` describing the current glyph (e.g. "Glyph editing canvas — {glyph}") without a role change, but wants a deliberate design call. Otherwise FM audited **a11y-strong** (S125): 0 nameless visible controls after the slider fix, icon buttons carry titles/aria, modal focus-trap via `aOpenModal`, `.me-coord`/`.ad-box` focus-visible indicators present, wrapping-`<label>` coordinate inputs. | found: 2026-07-20, S125 Pass C

- [~] Item (4) ✅ DONE 2026-07-29 (out-of-band, user-directed), b158af4; item (3) remains informational-only | torah_trainer.html | **Pass G print residuals** (S53). **(4) FIXED — side-by-side prints as parallel columns again.** S53 had collapsed it to one column because the two independent block flows broke at different points and produced **wrong verse pairings** (~15 verses out of sync) — truthful, but it cost the parallel text this layout exists for. Restored without the risk: in `@media print` the column wrappers become **`display:contents`** so every cell is a direct grid item, and the builder assigns each cell an **explicit `grid-row` shared across columns**, pinning a verse's Hebrew/translit/translation to the same row so they cannot drift wherever the page breaks. **The trap that made this non-trivial:** the Hebrew column also emits **aliyah dividers**, so it has more children than its siblings and any naive row-index alignment skews — the row map allocates a row per divider (spanning all columns). With no second text there is nothing to align, so that case keeps the single-column collapse rather than reserving an empty half-page. Screen is untouched (`grid-row`/`grid-column` are inert on non-grid-items; measured screen `grid-template-columns` identical before/after). **Method worth reusing: a standalone feasibility probe ran FIRST** — a throwaway page proving Chrome fragments an explicit-placement grid correctly (60 pairs, 4 pages, none split), which de-risked the whole change before a line of production code was written. Then a **negative control** (pre-fix 70/70 rows misaligned + 1 print column → post-fix 0/70 + 3 columns) and a 10-run × light/dark matrix covering 3-col, 2-col, no-parallel, interlinear and bible page; 12-page PDF generated and the rendered page inspected. **(3) still open, and still has no fix:** nikkud letter-mode colours print but collapse to similar greys on B/W — there is no secondary channel available for vowels the way the trope layer got its print underline.

- [x] 🚫 WON'T-FIX (maintainer decision, 2026-07-22 — accept as-is) | Hebrew_Font_Maker.html | **Non-dagesh per-letter shapes share the ccmp adjacency fragility** (S115, found during the shin-dagesh fix). **DECISION: documented known limitation — no reported symptom, and the full cure is a combinatorial pl-on-form glyph blowup; not worth the glyph-count cost without a real report. (The mechanical per-mark-filtered-lookup slice was explicitly NOT taken.)** The v5.5 `ccmp_dagesh` mark-filtered lookup fixed the dagesh class, but every `builtPerLetter` rule for a NON-dagesh mark (a per-letter *vowel* shape) still emits as a plain unfiltered `sub base mark by pl_…` — unmatchable when a canonically-earlier mark intervenes, AND unreachable when the base ligates first (a per-letter qamats on bet can never fire once בּ→FB31 — would need combinatorial pl-on-form glyphs). Same disease, wider cure: per-mark filtered lookups are mechanical, but the pl-on-form combinatorics are a design call (glyph-count blowup vs. accepting the global shape on dotted letters). No reported symptom. | found: 2026-07-20, S115
- [x] 🚫 ACCEPTED S141 (user-directed backlog close — measured-fine/parked, revisit only on a real report) · P4 (pre-existing limitation, documented) | Hebrew_Font_Maker.html | **Kern pairs never cover precomposed targets** (S115 observation). Kern sides resolve stored letter cps via `gname` (`kernSide`), so a pair against ש/ב never applies to the ligated uniFB2A/uniFB31 — true before v5.5 (FB2A) and inherited unchanged by FB2C/FB2D. A fix would mirror base-letter kern classes onto their form glyphs at FEA-emit time. Cosmetic-spacing scope. | found: 2026-07-20, S115

- [x] ✅ SHIPPED S162, 10bcbfa (gate-1 approved — maintainer chose **fronts + duplex backs** over the front-only split the seed suggested) | flash_cards.html | **Print a deck as a cut-out card sheet** (S159 Pass G seed). Setup-screen "Print card sheet" button lays the generated deck out **3×3 per page with dashed cut lines**; the `sheetDuplex` toggle appends an answer page after each card page. **The duplex mapping is the load-bearing part:** flipping on the long edge reverses left-right, so each ROW is reversed on the answer page, and a short final row is **padded to full width BEFORE reversing** — without the pad, a 2-card row reverses into the wrong columns and every answer on it lands behind the wrong card. One builder covers all modes because every card is `{heb, translit, …}`: Hebrew blends, numbers, colours (cell painted from `colorCss` + `print-color-adjust`; only a literal hex/rgb may reach the style attribute) and emoji all verified. **89 lines** — inside the ~150 ceiling even with duplex, so no split was needed. `sheetDuplex` wired into `getSettings()`/`applySettings()` with `??`, riding the already-registered `hebrewFlashCards_settings` blob (no new AllTools keys).

- [x] ✅ 2026-07-28 (pre-merge adversarial audit of the Learner Ladder — maintainer-requested, fixes committed on the same branch) | flash_cards.html + 3 CSV rewords | **The audit's headline: level 8 "Add Shva" was a functional no-op, green tests notwithstanding.** `generateCard` never allows shva as a syllable **nucleus** (Rule N1 — a `'sh'` pick is rerolled then hard-rejected); shva renders ONLY as the automatic *connector* on a non-vowelled position before the last vowel (בְּרָ). Level 8 was mode 1 (single position, always vowelled) and the blend levels pinned every position vowelled — so **no level in the shipped ladder ever displayed a shva**, level 8 drilled the identical distribution as level 7, and "All thirteen" in level 9 really showed twelve. **Why 184 green checks missed it: the deck-fidelity test asserted only "no marks outside the allowed set" (subset), never "the promised mark occurs" (occurrence) — a subset assertion is NOT an occurrence assertion; pair them.** FIXED per maintainer decisions (both gated): (1) **shva is now the bridge level** — mode 2 with `connector:true` (`_ladderOverrides` pins `vowelPositions:[mode]`, the app's own CV̊-CV pattern), moved after `allvowels` to sit directly before the two-letter blends it bridges into; every card now deterministically carries the connector shva on letter 1 (ids are stable storage keys, so the reorder renumbers labels only); the inert `'sh'` key is gone from every vowel set (`V_ALL` = 12 real keys). (2) **Auto-save banks new-best only** — the first attempt at a level always banks (a first fail is exactly the weakness signal) and retries bank only on an improved `bestPct`, so Try-Again grinding can no longer flood the 50-entry history cap and silently evict the teacher's own saved drills (the audit's second should-fix). Plus five hardenings from the same audit: the Save button re-asserts its "✓ Saved" state when `applyI18n` re-runs `showResults` on a language switch (the reset invited a duplicate manual save with a fresh `savedAt` that import de-dupe would never collapse); **`applySettings` font resolution now accepts My Fonts ids** (`allFonts()`/`allEngFonts()`, not the built-in lists alone) — previously ANY settings round trip (preset load, `.ivrit` restore, ladder exit) silently coerced an uploaded font back to the default and the ladder exit *committed* it, a pre-existing bug the ladder made routine; a loaded share-code inline word pool is stashed/restored across enter/exit (the round trip nulled it); `_ladderExit` catches a throwing restore and **skips** `saveSettings` on failure (disk already holds the correct pre-ladder blob — persisting a half-restored state was the only way to lose it); the level map's ▶ mirrors in RTL via the existing `dir-arrow` convention (✓/🔒 deliberately don't). Three level descriptions reworded (EN+HE, maintainer-approved): `desc_shva` (the connector pattern), `desc_allvowels` (drops the false "thirteen"), `desc_blend2a` (names its four vowels — the old "first four" contradicted its own key list, which swaps tzere for chirik by design). **Verified: suite upgraded to 214 checks incl. the missing class — occurrence assertions** (every shva-level card carries the connector on letter 1 across ~250 draws; all three hatafs appear in `allvowels`; non-connector levels stay shva-free), new-best-only banking counts, language-switch button state, a My-Fonts byte-identical round trip, word-pool restore, and a forced-throw exit that must strand nothing; report suite still 104/104; negative control still discriminates. Audit also re-confirmed all structural invariants independently (single `SETTINGS_KEY` write site, no chokepoint recursion, no XSS — a missing i18n key *drops* an interpolation rather than injecting it, letter groups exactly partition the 31-letter display set). Accepted/backlog (deliberately not this PR): `names3` fills card 10 with one repeat (9-letter pool, documented `generateDeck` stuck-pass); focus lands on `<body>` at drill start (identical to the existing Start-button path); duplicate word-list *names* collapse across any settings round trip (pre-existing `wlNameIndex` semantics); the manual-save variant of the language-switch button reset for non-ladder drills (pre-existing); `mergeProfilesBlob` throws re-importing a profile missing `results` (pre-existing, adjacent); `.ladder-card-pitch` muted-on-paper ≈4.4:1 light (house token pairing). `sw.js` stays v425 — that bump is on this unmerged branch and already covers these edits.

- [x] ✅ SHIPPED 2026-07-28 (user-directed), 7bff4f2 | flash_cards.html | **Learner Ladder — a guided decoding path.** Every drill in this tool is teacher-configured, so a learner opening the page alone had no answer to *"where do I start?"* and no sense of progress between sessions. A **🪜 Learning Path** card at the top of the setup screen opens a level map of **12 ordered levels** — letter names (3), then one vowel family at a time (6), then two- and three-letter blends (3) — each one tap: configure → drill → score → record → unlock the next at **80%** (deliberately the same threshold as `renderProfileHistory`'s ✓, so "passed" and "✓" never disagree). **No second deck generator and no pedagogy engine** — a level is just a locked-down configuration of the existing machinery, held in one `FC_LADDER` data array so content and mechanics stay separable. **The core of the change is settings safety.** `saveSettings()` has **~70 call sites**, so a level that reconfigures the drill would otherwise persist itself over the teacher's setup; following torah_trainer's `_handoutEnter`/`_handoutExit`, a level snapshots `getSettings()`, applies overrides, and restores on the way out with `saveSettings()` a **no-op for the duration**. **`showScreen()` turned out to be a 3-line chokepoint**, so ONE guard there covers every route home (header button, End Practice Early, Return to Options) instead of a restore sprinkled at each call site; `_ladderEnter` also unwinds if `startPractice` bails, which it can on an empty deck. Levels **pin** the deck-shaping fields and **inherit** presentation (font, colours, audio, translit style), so a snapshot carrying Other mode, a letter lock or listening can't quietly produce the wrong deck. **Blend levels pin `vowelPositions` to every position:** the app default (`{mode}`) puts a **shva connector** on the earlier letters (בְּרָ), and a first blend drill wants plain CV-CV (בָּרָ). **Progress lives inside the existing profile object** as `ladder`, so **no new localStorage key, no AllTools wiring, no migration** — `.ivrit` backup already exports profiles wholesale; **unlock state is derived from `bestPct`, never stored**, so no pointer can de-sync across a merge. **Two latent field-dropping merge sites found and fixed:** `renameProfile` and `mergeProfilesBlob` both rebuild a profile from `results` alone and silently dropped the new field — both now merge it (higher `bestPct` wins, **earliest** `passedAt` kept). **Two re-entrancy hazards handled:** Redo / Practice-Mistakes replay a deck that is no longer the level's own draw, so they **detach the scoring claim while keeping the settings binding** (restoring mid-deck would swap the configuration out from under the open cards); and the record + auto-save are guarded because **`applyI18n` re-runs `showResults` on a language switch**, which must not bank the same drill twice. `saveCurrentResult`'s write step is extracted as **`saveResultToProfile`** so the ladder's auto-save shares it rather than forking storage — ladder drills then feed weakness tracking and the progress report for free. **43** new `flashcards.ladder.*` keys (EN+HE, **gate-2 approved**; one planned key dropped in favour of reusing `results.save_button_done`). **Gate 1 (the 12-level progression + letter/vowel groupings) was also maintainer-approved** — pedagogy is the maintainer's call, not the implementer's. Verified headless (`serviceWorkers:'block'`, external origins aborted): **184 checks, 0 pageerror** — settings **byte-identical** across a full multi-level session, pass/fail/replay-lower paths, both merge sites, locked-row + focus behaviour, no-profile flow, `startPractice` bail-out, XSS probe, EN+HE with RTL **measured**, light/dark × 1280/800. **Deck fidelity is asserted on the rendered nikkud** (~150 draws per level) after the first attempt passed **vacuously** — cards are only `{heb, translit}`, so the intended `vowelKeys`/`letterHebs` reads were `undefined`; the rewrite classifies U+05BC by context (dagesh in בּ vs. **shuruk** in וּ) and is backed by a **negative control** proving it detects injected violations. The progress-report suite still passes **104/104** after the refactor. `sw.js` v424→v425.

- [x] ✅ SHIPPED 2026-07-28 (user-directed), 68dc30f | flash_cards.html | **Printable one-page student progress report.** Teacher Tools' profile detail gains a **📄 Progress Report** button that prints a parent-conference-ready sheet built entirely from `hebrewFlashCards_profiles` — **no new collection, schema change, network call, persistent state, AllTools key or CSP change** (so no preset/`.ivrit` wiring gate). **Architecture copies this page's OWN print precedent, the S162 card sheet (`printCardSheet`), not torah_trainer's handout:** a static print-only host `#fcReportSheet` beside `#fcSheet`, a `body.fc-report-print` class, and an **`afterprint`** teardown — `_reportEnter`/`_reportExit` are top-level + idempotent so headless tests can drive them without blocking on the dialog, and `afterprint` rather than `try/finally` avoids a blank page where `print()` returns early (mobile Safari). Sections: header (name via `esc()`, generated date, period) · summary (drills / cards / overall accuracy / best) · focus areas · strengths · recent-drills `<table>` · teacher comments + signature + storage-locality line. Focus and Strengths share **one row cascade** (`combos` → `letters`+`vowels` → `other`) so a report never mixes granularities; reuses `analyzeWeaknesses`, `weakRows`, `describeResultMode`, `esc`, `announce` and `renderProfileHistory`'s ✓/–/✗ thresholds so screen and paper agree. **The streak / personal best is deliberately omitted** — `PB_STREAK_KEY` is device-global, not per-profile, so printing it beside a student's name would be wrong data on a document handed to a parent. **Two print bugs found while verifying and fixed:** `body{min-height:100vh}` pinned the body to a full page box, and `main.wrap`'s `padding:…100px` survived its hidden children (114px of dead space) — together they pushed a fitting report onto a blank second sheet; the fix hides the **wrapper**, not just `.screen`. - [x] 2026-07-27 | 2eb5775 | CLAUDE.md | **Offline/failure tests must open the context with `serviceWorkers: 'block'`** (S165 — the session's most reusable finding). `sw.js` calls `skipWaiting()` + `clients.claim()`, so the worker activates on the **first** load and then serves `/data/` from the version-independent `DATA_CACHE` — and **service-worker requests bypass Playwright's `page.route`**. A route that aborts `/data/` therefore stops blocking the moment the worker is live: the fetch succeeds, no failure path runs, and the test reports the happy path while looking like it tested failure. Added to the Playwright recipe with the one-line fix.

- [x] 2026-07-27 | (S165 — NO CHANGE NEEDED) | flash_cards.html | **"Flash Cards is silent when its data fails" was a probe artifact, not a defect.** Pass F's first run reported it, and the maintainer picked it at gate 1 over the queued feature. Before writing any code I instrumented every write to `#wordsCount` and found `ALL_WORDS_DATA` **populated** — the block had never held (the service worker was serving the corpus), and the "0 words in pool" text was simply correct for the default `wordsSource === 'mylists'` with no saved lists. Re-tested with `serviceWorkers: 'block'` and `wordsSource = 'alldict'`: Flash Cards shows **"Failed to load dictionary words." / "טעינת מילות המילון נכשלה."** — a proper localized message. **No code changed.** The value of the iteration was catching it before shipping a fix for a non-bug, and the harness rule it produced (2eb5775).

- [x] 2026-07-27 | 9ba18e5 | torah_trainer.html | **The reading area showed developer key names when data was blocked** (S165 Pass F; P2). `torah.reading.empty_title`, `torah.reading.empty_body` and `torah.status.pick_parsha` rendered literally, **in both languages** — the **third instance** of the `pre-ready-i18n / never-re-rendered` pattern (registered S152, hit again S158). Failure-path only, which is why earlier passes missed it: when the data loads, the empty state is written *after* `I18n.ready`. `applyI18n` did re-render the reading — but only when one was actually loaded, and the empty state was exactly the branch it skipped. **FIXED:** the card is repaired **in place** rather than via `renderEmptyReading()`, because that fires `_tourOnReadingChanged()` → `_tourShow()`, which advances a running tour, and `applyI18n` must never disturb tour/audio state. The status line needed more: healing the stuck key leaves the line in the **old language** after a later switch, so `setStatus(msg, isError, key)` now optionally records its key and `applyI18n` re-resolves from it; interpolated messages (the "Failed to load {label}" family) record no key and are untouched — verified by the blocked=false rows where the empty card shows but the status is a real message. **Verified:** blocked and unblocked × EN/HE × light/dark — 0 raw keys, 0 pageerror; EN→HE→EN round trip moves both card and status and returns them intact; pre-fix replay under the corrected harness reproduces all three keys.

- [x] 2026-07-27 | 02f4ed0 | hebrew_dictionary.html | **The Dictionary hung on "Loading dictionary…" forever when the network blocked its corpus** (S165 Pass F; **P2, and the only tool of seven that crashed**). The corpus fetch is kicked off at **parse time** while `js/i18n.js` is **deferred**, so a fast failure lands in `.catch()` before `I18n` exists — and a bare `I18n.t()` on an undeclared global **throws a ReferenceError**, killing the handler before it could set the message or hide the progress bar. The teacher got a permanent spinner and an uncaught error. **FIXED:** rather than only guarding the call, the handler re-points the element's `data-i18n` at the failure key and paints the English fallback immediately — correct under **both** orderings (I18n already up → right language at once; I18n later → `applyStaticI18n()` fills it, and a later language switch re-translates it, which a plain guard would not). Fallback is the CSV's own `en` value, so **no new copy**. **Verified:** data blocked × EN/HE × light/dark, **plus the same matrix with `js/i18n.js` deliberately delayed 1.2 s past the failure** to force the pre-ready ordering — all 8 show the correct localized message, bar hidden, 0 pageerror; happy path unaffected (loader hides, 100 result cards); pre-fix replay reproduces the ReferenceError.

- [x] 2026-07-27 | (S165 Pass F) | all 7 tools | **Cross-tool consistency — 14th run. Affordance: behaviour when the tool's data fetch fails.** Chosen because it is the teachers' real environment (filtered school wifi, captive portals, offline) and had never been swept. Contract compared: no `pageerror`, a visible message, localized, not a dead end. **Result: 2 of 7 broken, both fixed** (dictionary crash, torah raw keys) — **5 fine.** **The exemplar to converge on is `trope_tutor.html`**: *"Example clips unavailable (couldn't load the trope index) — Learn mode still shows every mark."* — it names what failed, what still works, and is fully localized. The generator and Font Maker degrade gracefully with intact empty states; Flash Cards and the dashboard both carry real localized failure messages (`flashcards.words.load_failed`, `dashboard.weather.unavailable`, `dashboard.days.shabbat_unavailable`). **Method caveat that shaped the whole pass:** the first run's verdicts for Flash Cards and the dashboard were **worthless** — lazy fetches were never triggered, and the service worker was defeating the route block entirely (see 2eb5775).

- **ivrit-gather-gap** (a store the tool's `IVRIT_CFG.apply()` is fully able to restore, but `gather()` never writes — so the tool's own `.ivrit` backup silently omits it, while the index.html AllTools bundle carries it correctly): **registered + swept 2026-07-25 (S159, Pass G), hits: 2 of the 3 `.ivrit` tools.** (1) **flash_cards — student profiles, P1 data-loss (FIXED d7a6f23):** `apply()` reads `data.flashCardProfiles` and calls `mergeProfilesBlob`, with a comment saying it restores profiles "when the file includes them" — `gather()` never included them, so the file carried `profileFolders` **with none of the profiles inside**. Restoring on a new machine returned empty folders and lost every student's drill history. (2) **generator — `hebFont` + `livePreview`, P3 (FIXED 3a06669):** `getSettings()` carried `hebFontSize` but not the font itself, so a restored worksheet used the right size in the default face. (3) **dashboard — CLEAN** (its settings blob is exactly what `liveState` already carries). clean streak: 0 — **ACTIVE, consequence-critical** (backup completeness / data-loss-on-new-machine, the same carve-out as localStorage-vs-AllTools). **Why the existing sweep missed it:** localStorage-vs-AllTools checks keys against **index.html's three functions**, and both gaps were correctly registered *there* — the per-tool `.ivrit` payload is a second, independent registry that nothing was checking. **Detection (cheap, headless):** for each page with an `IVRIT_CFG`, call `IVRIT_CFG.gather()` live and diff `Object.keys(result)` + `Object.keys(result.liveState)` against the keys that tool owns in the AllTools table; anything owned-but-absent is a candidate. Then confirm by round-trip — seed real state, export, wipe, `IVRIT_CFG.apply(parsed.data, 'merge')`, and assert the state came back. **Read `apply()` first:** in both hits `apply()` was already correct, so the whole defect was one missing line in `gather()`. **Re-swept 2026-07-29 (S168), hits: 0** — all four `IVRIT_CFG` carriers checked; `apply()` and `gather()` are aligned, and the apparent gaps are the documented AllTools-bundle **alias** keys (`data.presets || data.flashCardPresets`). Specifically confirmed that the **Learner Ladder rides inside the profile blob that `gather()` writes**, so a FlashCards `.ivrit` carries a student's whole ladder. **Detector bug fixed mid-sweep:** slicing the gather body at `'apply('` truncates on a *comment* that mentions apply(), which hid `flashCardProfiles` and produced a false gap.
- **symbol-only accessible name** (an icon-only control whose ENTIRE accessible name is a glyph with no letter or digit — "×", "↺", "✕", "⬛", an emoji run — because for `button`/`a`/`role=button` the ACCNAME chain takes **name-from-content BEFORE `title`**, so a correct translated `title` on the very same element is never reached. A screen reader announces "multiplication sign, button" or, for an unmapped glyph, nothing): **registered + swept suite-wide 2026-07-29 (S167, Pass C), hits: 50 across 6 tools** — dashboard 27, font-maker 13, generator 4, flash-cards 5, dictionary 3, torah 2; trope_tutor, index, resources, contact, privacy and 404 **clean**. **All 50 fixed** (ddc9180 + 2c4b78f): 38 were pure wiring (mirror the existing translated `title` into `aria-label`), 12 had no label at all and took 9 new keys + 3 reuses. clean streak: 0 — **ACTIVE, consequence-critical** (a11y blocker, and it re-appears every time someone adds an icon button). **Detection:** compute the ACCNAME chain in-page over the focusable set and flag `name && !/\p{L}|\p{N}/u.test(name)` — but run it BOTH ways, because each method misses what the other catches: the runtime sweep missed the generator's two collapsed-section resets and every overlay-only button, while a static `<button ...>INNER</button>` scan missed all 4 dashboard sites built by the `mk()` helper and needed the modals driven open to be believed. **Two exemptions, deliberate:** a `div[role="tab"]`/`role="button"` whose content is real text IS named (name-from-content) — a hand-rolled shim that only allows content for `button`/`a` reports these as nameless, which is how S167 briefly "found" a nonexistent Font-Maker defect; and a glyph label that is genuinely decorative beside visible text is fine. **Fix rule:** when mirroring inside a JS builder, gate it on the label having no letters, so a worded button keeps its visible text as its name (WCAG 2.5.3 Label in Name).
- nameless-adjacent-text-labeled control (a visible interactive control — toggle switch, slider, number field, select, colour well — whose only label is **adjacent text** that is never programmatically associated, so it has NO accessible name; the wrapping `<label class="toggle"/"switch">` holds just the input + its slider span, and non-checkbox inputs have no `<label for>`): **re-swept 2026-07-25 (S158, Pass A): 0 new hits; the 4 residual FIXED (74c2fcd) → the suite is now at 0 nameless interactive controls.** clean streak: **1** — KEPT ACTIVE (consequence-critical). **Detector caveat learned S158:** force-unhiding `.hide`/`display:none` can expose a control that is *only* nameless while hidden — Font Maker's `#nodeEditToggle` is built empty and labelled by `updateNodeEditToggle()` in the same synchronous call that reveals it, so it is named whenever visible; treat a hidden-only hit as a false positive after checking the reveal path. Original sweep: **registered + swept 2026-07-25 (S157, Pass C), hits: 122 across the whole suite** — generator 90, flash_cards 31, dictionary 1; dashboard / torah / trope / index / resources already at **0** from the S135 / S146 / earlier C passes. **118 fixed** (1cd1362, c91a121, fdce7c9); the 4 residual flash_cards min/max spinbuttons are blocked on a gate-2 copy decision, not on code. clean streak: 0 — **ACTIVE, consequence-critical** (a11y blocker: a screen-reader user hears "checkbox" with nothing to say WHICH option, on the tools' primary settings surfaces). **Detection (re-usable, and the important lesson):** read the **real Chromium AX tree** via CDP `Accessibility.enable` + `Accessibility.getFullAXTree`, filter to interactive roles (`checkbox`/`slider`/`spinbutton`/`combobox`/`ColorWell`/`textbox`), and flag `!node.name.value`. A hand-rolled JS name shim **over-reports badly** (S157's first pass claimed 45 false positives — `role="button"` divs like the letter tiles ARE named from content, which only the real AX algorithm knows). **Two traps worth remembering:** (1) **hidden surface hides hits** — the generator showed 8 nameless until collapsed panels were expanded AND `display:none` sidebar rows unhidden, then 90; flash_cards showed 14 until `.hide` blocks were force-opened, then 31. Always force the full control surface open before counting. (2) **non-empty ≠ correct** — verify each computed name: both the generator's and flash_cards' TTS sliders picked up **"Slow"**, the range's low-end caption, and a naive row-scoped `aria-labelledby` can swallow a tooltip's whole body into the name. Check for names >48 chars and eyeball the list. **RE-OPENED 2026-07-27 (S164): NOT clean in `Hebrew_Font_Maker.html`.** The S157 sweep and S125's `vSlider()` pass both missed sliders built **by hand** rather than through the helper: **20 nameless `input[type=range]`** in the mark, precomposed-form and anchor panels. **13 fixed (18067f0)** — those whose visible localized label sits on the slider's own line, so the name reuses that exact `I18n.t()` call; **7 remain** (label lives elsewhere, one still hardcoded English) and are logged as a candidate. **Detection lesson:** auditing rendered sliders alone under-counts badly — the owning panels only render with specific state (a selected mark, a precomp target, the anchor stage), so an initial headless sweep saw 12 sliders and reported 1 nameless when the static truth was 20. Sweep the source AND drive each owning render function. clean streak: **0** — ACTIVE. **RE-SWEPT + CLOSED 2026-07-29 (S166):** the 7 residual Font Maker sliders are named (3219909), so a static scan of all 35 `input[type=range]` in that file finds **0 without `aria-label`/`aria-labelledby`/`title`**, and the real AX tree shows **0 nameless of 17 rendered** across EN/HE × light/dark × 1280/800. **The suite is back to 0 nameless interactive controls.** clean streak: **1**. **Detector lessons added (all three produced a false clean before being caught):** panel builders in Font Maker take **arguments** (`renderTraceLeft(L, l)`, `renderWideFormControls(pc)`) — calling them bare throws into a `catch` and the sweep silently reports the handful of always-rendered sliders as the whole population; `project.letters` is an **Array** of objects carrying `.codepoint`, not a codepoint-keyed map; and **top-level `let` globals cannot be assigned from `page.evaluate`** (the assignment lands on `window`, which the lexical binding shadows) — drive state through `_selectItem`/`_setWorkMode` instead. Corollary to the standing rule: **a sweep that reports "0 hits" must also report how many controls it actually saw.**
- dead-feature-guard (a `typeof X === 'function'` / feature-detection guard whose **preferred** implementation does not exist on that page, so the guarded branch can never run and control silently falls through to a worse path — or to nothing): **re-swept 2026-07-25 (S158, Pass A): 0 NEW hits** (23 distinct guards across 16 files; only the registered-benign generator `saveSettings` remains, and S156's fix incidentally REMOVED the `renderCustomWordlist` guard — so 3 of the original 4 are now resolved). clean streak: **1**. **Detector caveat learned S158:** a `typeof X === 'function'` check on a **function parameter** (`js/i18n.js` `onChange(fn)`) is a false positive — the declaration regex looks for `function X(`/`const X =`/`X =`, which a parameter never matches. Skip names bound as a parameter of the enclosing function. Original sweep: **registered + swept 2026-07-24 (S155, Pass F), hits: 4 of 25 non-external guards.** (1) dictionary `showAppToast` → always `alert()`ed — **FIXED 0f60983**; (2) generator `renderRealWords` → translit restyle was a **no-op on Real Words worksheets** — real **P2**, **FIXED S156 (9e95399)** after the S155 attempt was reverted; the fix also had to clear a second, non-guard defect (a stale shallow-copy `rwRenderCache`), so a dead guard can mask more than one bug; (3) generator `renderCustomWordlist` → doubly dead (its `cwModeActive` condition is a documented retired flag, always false) — harmless; (4) generator `saveSettings` in `setVowelScheme` → dead but harmless (the generator has no settings-blob autosave; `vowelColorScheme` persists via `getSettings()` → presets/`.ivrit`). clean streak: 0 — **ACTIVE** (S156: 2 of the 4 registered hits now fixed; the 2 remaining are the documented-benign ones) (a dead guard is invisible: no error, no console warning, and the code *reads* as if the good path runs). **Detection:** regex every `typeof (window.)?NAME === 'function'`, drop an allowlist of genuinely-external globals (browser APIs + CDN libs), then check whether NAME is declared/assigned anywhere in that file. **Known false positive:** a guard on a **function parameter** (`js/i18n.js` `onChange(fn)`) — correct validation, not a dead branch; exclude params before reporting. **Re-swept 2026-07-29 (S168): 1 HIT of 27 guards inspected** — the generator's `setVowelScheme` guarded a `saveSettings()` the file defines zero times (a copy of flash cards', where it exists). **Fixed 766f723** by replacing the line with a comment rather than deleting it silently, since the dead guard misled in both directions. clean streak: **0** — ACTIVE.
- **modal-focus-trap** (an element declaring `role="dialog"` + `aria-modal="true"` — which tells assistive tech the rest of the page is inert — with **no Tab/Shift+Tab wrap**, so keyboard focus walks out behind the dialog onto controls the AT has been told do not exist; worst on a full-viewport overlay, where the escaped focus ring is not visible anywhere): **registered + swept suite-wide 2026-07-26 (S161, Pass I), 2 hits of 9 dialogs, both fixed (ec41bb0).** Detection: enumerate every `aria-modal="true"` container, open each **via its real opener** (forcing `.open`/`display` skips the wiring and gives a false result — and an unpopulated dialog under-reports its focusables), focus the first focusable inside, then press Tab `n+6` times and Shift+Tab the same, counting frames where `document.activeElement.closest(container)` is null. **Hits: `#frModal` (dashboard first-run card) and `#pickOverlay` (dashboard student picker).** Clean: dashboard settings drawer, dictionary word modal, torah + trope settings drawers, index gear modal, Font Maker (global `_activeModal` trap covers its 14 dialogs). Clean streak: **1**. **KEPT ACTIVE regardless of streak is NOT claimed** — this is accessibility-scoped, not security/data-loss, so it auto-retires at 3 clean sweeps per the rule. **Note for the next sweep:** a dialog that only appears for a brand-new user (first-run cards) is invisible to Pass C, which always runs with populated storage — sweep this pattern from a **fresh context**. **Re-swept 2026-07-29 (S168): CLEAN at runtime.** Static counting cannot decide this class (a trap may be written `e.key !== 'Tab'`, or live in a shared helper), so it was measured: the dashboard settings drawer, torah settings drawer and trope settings drawer all hold focus through 30 tabs, and the dashboard **student picker** holds it through 6 when tested in isolation. **Two false alarms on the way, both mine:** filtering candidates on `offsetParent !== null` finds nothing at all, because **`offsetParent` is `null` for `position: fixed`** — which every one of these drawers is; and probing `[aria-modal=true]` with `.pop()` can latch onto a *second* visible dialog (the first-run `frModal`) and report the wrong element leaking. Test one dialog per page load.
- pre-ready-i18n / never-re-rendered (an `I18n.t()` call during init — before `I18n.ready` resolves — writing text or an attribute onto a node that the page's `applyI18n` never re-renders, so the returned **raw key string** sticks permanently, in **both** languages): **re-swept 2026-07-25 (S158, Pass A): 19 NEW hits, all fixed (790ca80)** — torah_trainer's 13 vowel + 6 trope colour inputs, whose `aria-label` came from `I18n.t()` inside `initColorPickers`/`initTropeColorPickers` at init while `applyI18n` rebuilt six *other* things but not those two lists. **Hebrew-only this time** (English resolves pre-ready), which is why the S152 sweep — and every pass since — missed it: an EN-only raw-key scan cannot see this. **Sweep both languages.** clean streak: 0 — ACTIVE, consequence-critical. **Fix shape to reuse:** when a builder calls `I18n.t()`, either re-call that builder from `applyI18n` (correct when the builder is a pure re-render, as here) or use flash_cards' English-fallback + `data-i18n-aria-label` pattern — but the latter cannot carry `{param}` substitution, so a parameterised name forces the re-call. Original: **registered + swept 2026-07-24 (S152, Pass B), hits: 44** — 42 tooltip triggers whose `aria-label` was the literal `shared.tip.aria_more_info` (generator 31 / dictionary 5 / torah 5 / trope 1) + 2 visible counters (`#pickerRosterCount` showing `dashboard.picker.roster_count.other`, `#exportCount` showing `dictionary.export.count.other`). All 44 fixed (02693b6, caaa0fc); **flash_cards already carried the correct pattern** (English fallback + `data-i18n-aria-label`, translated by the on-ready `applyStaticI18n` sweep) and is the canonical reference. clean streak: 0 — **ACTIVE, consequence-critical** (a11y-blocking + user-visible wrong text, AND invisible to both existing gates: the runtime's `dictLoaded` warn-gate deliberately suppresses the `[i18n] miss` console warning pre-ready — see the comment in `js/i18n.js` — and `check-i18n` is static-only, so it cannot see a runtime timing bug). **Detection (re-usable):** headless raw-key scan over all 12 pages × EN/HE — body text nodes (excluding `SCRIPT`/`STYLE`/`TEMPLATE`) plus `title`/`aria-label`/`placeholder`/`alt` — matching a page-prefix key regex (`shared|index|generator|dashboard|flashcards|dictionary|torah|trope|fontmaker|resources|contact|privacy|notfound|pwa` + `.`-segments); post-fix scan = **0** suite-wide. **3rd instance 2026-07-27 (S165, Pass F): torah_trainer's failure path.** The empty-reading card and the status line are written before `I18n.ready` whenever a data fetch fails fast; `applyI18n` re-rendered the reading **only when one was loaded**, so the empty branch kept its raw keys in both languages. Fixed in place (never via `renderEmptyReading()` — that advances a running tour). **New sub-lesson:** healing a stuck key from the *visible text* is not enough — the line then stops following language switches; record the key at write time (`setStatus(msg, isError, key)`) and re-resolve from it. clean streak: **0** — ACTIVE.
- _(**shadowed-global helper RETIRED at S73** — 3 consecutive clean sweeps (registered S40 w/ 2 torah hits fixed, then clean S64 + S73 over the i18n rollout: one `esc`/`applyI18n` per file; FM `t()` + `pwa.js` `t()` are intentional in-scope locals); correctness-scoped, not consequence-critical → auto-retired. Re-checked only in Pass A2. Also **listener/interval accumulation** + **JSON-LD ↔ visible-content parity** RETIRED at S64; workMode/step reachability RETIRED at S52; undo-wiring + slider-commit RETIRED at S36 — see Retired patterns.)_
- _**Retirement rule (as applied):** a pattern retires after 3 consecutive clean sweeps **UNLESS it is consequence-critical** (security or data-loss). The three that hit 3-clean at S64 — unescaped-input/unsafe-parse (XSS), localStorage-vs-AllTools (backup), destructive-bulk (teacher data) — are **deliberately kept ACTIVE**: new code reintroduces them anytime and they re-sweep cheaply, so the safety margin outweighs the noise. Only structural/cosmetic classes auto-retire. Retired patterns are re-checked only in pass A2._

- **placeholder-as-only-accessible-name** (a text input/textarea whose ONLY name source is its `placeholder` — no `aria-label`, `aria-labelledby`, `label[for]`, wrapping `<label>` or `title`. Under ACCNAME `placeholder` is the last-resort source, so the name is announced on an empty field and then **vanishes once the field holds a value** — i.e. exactly while the teacher is using it. **Detection:** headless, per page, over `input:not([type=hidden]):not([type=checkbox]):not([type=radio]):not([type=button]):not([type=submit]), textarea` that carry a `placeholder`; walk the ACCNAME precedence chain in-page and count anything landing on `PLACEHOLDER-ONLY`. A static grep for `<input …placeholder…>` without `aria-label` **over-reports** — it can't see `label[for]` or a wrapping `<label>`): **registered + swept 2026-07-26 (S163), hits: 17 across 6 tools, ALL FIXED (4451a41).** By tool — dashboard 6, flash_cards 4, generator 2, dictionary 2, font-maker 2, index 1; **clean:** torah_trainer, trope_tutor, resources, contact, privacy (all already named via `aria-label`/`label[for]`/wrapping label). clean streak: **0** — ACTIVE. Consequence: accessibility blocker, cheap to reintroduce (every new text input is a chance), so keep ACTIVE.
- **class-only-selected-state** (a mutually-exclusive selector button that marks the current choice with a CSS `.active` class and **no `aria-pressed`/`aria-checked`**, so assistive tech announces every sibling identically and the user cannot tell which option is live. **Detection:** grep `?'active':''` template toggles **plus** runtime `classList.toggle('active', …)` setters, then confirm headless that every rendered `.mode-btn`/`.mark-sq`/`.count-btn` agrees `class .active ⟺ aria-pressed="true"`. **Two traps:** (a) the same button class is used for plain **action** buttons in FM — `aria-pressed` on those would be wrong, so judge per control, not per class; (b) buttons inside an **unopened modal** carry pre-sync markup and must be excluded — judging them manufactures phantom hits): **registered + swept 2026-07-26 (S163, Pass H), hits: 20, ALL FIXED** — 19 in `Hebrew_Font_Maker.html` (03340be: 15 template-literal + 4 static custom-glyph toggles) + 1 in `flash_cards.html` (07bebf7: the ten deck-size buttons). **Swept clean:** generator, dashboard, dictionary, torah_trainer, trope_tutor, index, resources (0 `?'active':''` toggles; 6–28 `aria-pressed` uses each). clean streak: **0** — ACTIVE. **RE-SWEPT 2026-07-29 (S167, Pass C): 2 live groups found, both logged rather than fixed — the variety governor blocked them** (flash_cards was at its 2-iteration cap when the finding landed). (a) `.weak-tab` ×4 in the flash_cards Weaknesses view: `aria-pressed` measured `null` on all four in every matrix run, so AT announces the four tabs identically; the fix is one line in `paintTabs()`, which already loops them to repaint. (b) ✅ **DONE S168, 83ed5c8** — `#ivritModeAuto`/`#ivritModeManual` in the shared `.ivrit` engine; it turned out `index.html` already carried the correct wiring and the other three carriers had drifted behind it, so the fix was to propagate the known-good copy (all four byte-identical again, sha 1755507c). clean streak: **0 — ACTIVE**. **Detection note:** grep for `.active`/`.selected` toggling is not enough — assert `aria-pressed`/`aria-checked`/`aria-selected` is non-null at runtime on each group, since the state is often painted by a JS repaint function rather than markup. **Re-swept 2026-07-29 (S168): 1 group FIXED (83ed5c8), 2 still open.** Fixed: the shared `.ivrit` Automatic/Manual buttons — and the diagnosis matters, because it was **drift, not omission**: `index.html` already had the correct `aria-pressed` wiring and the other three carriers had fallen behind, so the fix was to propagate the known-good copy and restore byte-identity (sha 1755507c). Still open: `.weak-tab` ×4 (flash_cards), and — **new, found this session** — the **Default/TaL AM vowel-scheme buttons in all four scheme tools**, which is now the top S169 candidate. clean streak: **0** — ACTIVE.

### Retired patterns

- falsy-zero (`s.field || default` silently discarding a stored `0`/`''`/`false` in a numeric/boolean restore) | retired 2026-07-17 (S93) | 3 consecutive clean sweeps: 1 hit S64 (FM `spec.version||1.0`, b9c1aa3), then clean S73, S83 (FM v4.18→v4.26 slider/geometry guards), S93 (dashboard movable-panels — array-order `panelLayout`, no numeric restores). Correctness-scoped (a wrong restored value, not data-loss) → not a consequence-critical carve-out → auto-retired. Re-checked only in Pass A2. **Watch:** any new tool with numeric/boolean `applySettings`/size-restore should re-register it if `|| default` reappears on a count/size/index/toggle.
- shadowed-global helper (a top-level helper — `esc`/`status`/`applyI18n`/`t`/… — shadowed by an inner decl so a global-expecting call site gets the wrong one) | retired 2026-07-13 (S73) | 3 consecutive clean sweeps: registered S40 (2 torah `esc`-shadow hits fixed, d88fa99), clean S64, clean S73 over the site-wide i18n rollout (one `esc`/`applyI18n` per file; FM's local `t()` in `shortcutGroups()` and `pwa.js`'s self-contained `t(key,fallback)` are intentional in-scope locals, never reach a global-`t` call site). Correctness-scoped, not consequence-critical → auto-retired. Re-checked only in Pass A2.
- listener/interval accumulation (a `setInterval`/`setTimeout`/`addEventListener` attached repeatedly without clear/remove) | retired 2026-07-10 (S64) | 3 consecutive clean sweeps (S52, and S64 over the S53–S63 surface — generator chunked-build timer single/self-chaining + `cancelWorksheetBuild` clears on re-entry, `beforeprint` attached once, torah color fns add no listeners, `_appToastTimer` clear-guarded). Structural/perf-scoped → auto-retired. Re-checked only in Pass A2.
- JSON-LD ↔ visible-content parity (an `application/ld+json` FAQ/HowTo/ItemList claim drifting from the visible UI or code constants) | retired 2026-07-10 (S64) | 3 consecutive clean sweeps (S52, and S64 — no `ld+json` block changed across S53–S63; spot-checked torah/generator/trope/FM claims still accurate). SEO/cosmetic-scoped → auto-retired. **Re-check ownership moved from Pass A2 → Pass L (SEO & discoverability) on L's first run, S142 2026-07-24** (per the L-subsumes-JSON-LD-parity rule): L re-verified every block parses and every FAQ/HowTo/ItemList claim matches the visible UI (index `ItemList` = 8 tools; generator/torah/trope/dict/FM FAQ+HowTo all accurate; newer features like dictionary themes / torah copy simply aren't enumerated, nothing claimed is stale) — **CLEAN**. A2 no longer re-checks this class.
- workMode/step reachability (controls reachable in only one workMode/step while the workflow steers users past it) | retired 2026-07-10 (S52) | 3 consecutive clean sweeps (S36, S41-scoped, S52 — trope_tutor's drawer/tabs/tour-skip/mid-drill-return all reachable-by-design). Re-checked only in Pass A2.
- undo-wiring (Font Maker: `markDirty()` without `udDo`/`udBurstBegin`/`udNudgeTick`) | retired 2026-07-08 (S36) | 3 consecutive clean sweeps; the S25–26 keyboard additions (node-insert, crop) verified as routing through `udDo` or deliberately non-undoable. Re-checked only in Pass A2. **Re-verified CLEAN 2026-07-09 (S41)** over the un-swept FM v3.9–v4.2 additions (auto-detect Apply via `udDo(...withSource)`, pen contour, node delete/paste/transform/specks/fillet all `udDo`; `_hiddenContours`/opacity/snapGuides are documented view-only).
- slider-commit (Font Maker: `oninput` range sliders lacking `onchange="udBurstCommit()"`) | retired 2026-07-08 (S36) | 3 consecutive clean sweeps; every project-data slider commits, and no new range slider has been added. Re-checked only in Pass A2. **Re-verified CLEAN 2026-07-09 (S41)** over the FM v3.9–v4.2 sliders (fillet `filletLiveInput`/`filletCommit` burst; size setters commit; mark-editor `meSetDotSize` uses its own `meBeginEdit`/`meCommitEdit` stack; `adSep`/`adTh` are detection-only until Apply).

## Recurring-pattern sweep status

- **S165 (2026-07-27): `pre-ready-i18n / never-re-rendered` hit a 3rd time, and the test harness itself was found unsound for failure paths.** Pass F swept failure behaviour across all seven tools: the dictionary's parse-time fetch threw a ReferenceError in its own `.catch` (deferred `js/i18n.js`), and torah_trainer's empty state showed raw keys because `applyI18n` re-rendered the reading only when one was loaded. **Two sub-lessons worth keeping.** (1) Healing a stuck key from the *visible text* is a half-fix — the line then stops following language switches; record the key at write time and re-resolve from it. (2) **Service workers defeat `page.route`**: `sw.js` uses `skipWaiting()`+`clients.claim()`, so `/data/` comes from `DATA_CACHE` and never reaches the route handler. Any offline/failure test must open the context with `serviceWorkers: 'block'`; without it the test reports the happy path while appearing to test failure. This manufactured a "Flash Cards is silent" finding that the maintainer then chose at gate 1 — caught before any code was written, by instrumenting the writes instead of trusting the observation. **Every previous route-abort-based "offline" result in this ledger should be treated as unverified for the same reason.**

- **S164 (2026-07-27): the S157 `nameless-adjacent-text-labeled control` pattern was NOT clean — re-opened.** Pass E itself came back 6/6 with one licences finding, but chasing the S163 mark-editor candidate surfaced **20 nameless `input[type=range]` in the Font Maker**: the mark, precomposed-form and anchor panels build sliders by hand, so both S125's `vSlider()` pass and S157's sweep missed them. 13 fixed from their own on-line label (zero new copy); 7 logged for per-control decisions. **The detection lesson is the valuable part:** an initial headless audit of *rendered* sliders saw 12 and reported **1** nameless — the owning panels only render with specific state (selected mark / precomp target / anchor stage). Static-scan the source **and** drive each owning render function; a rendered-only sweep under-counted 20 as 1. Second method note, from the licences dimension: neither a loose substring test nor an exact-hostname test is correct — the loose one cleared the real gap (`files.readme.io`, on the word "files") and the strict one flagged five origins that are documented by service name. Confirm each by hand.

- **S163 (2026-07-26): two NEW patterns registered and swept to zero.** (1) `placeholder-as-only-accessible-name` — the S161 P4 recorded 2 instances; the real class was **17 across 6 tools**, found by computing the ACCNAME precedence chain in-page on all 11 pages rather than grepping. All fixed with **zero new CSV keys** (gate-2: "reuse existing strings everywhere"), names verified in Chromium's real AX tree **with a value typed in**, EN+HE. (2) `class-only-selected-state` — 20 selector buttons (19 font-maker, 1 flash_cards) whose selected state was a CSS class only; all fixed, all other tools swept clean. Pass L ran first and was structurally clean (see the Done entry). **Method notes from this session — the harness manufactured FOUR false findings before any were believed:** (a) an attribute regex must **backreference the opening quote**, or an apostrophe inside a `"…"` value truncates the match (this alone invented "trope_tutor description = 40 chars"); (b) `decode(null) → ''` conflated *absent* with *present-but-empty* and invented 4 phantom empty-tag findings; (c) reading Chromium's AX tree for a control inside a **collapsed panel** returns "not in AX tree" — reveal the ancestor chain first, and note a `type=number` input silently rejects a non-numeric test value; (d) stripping `.overlay.open` on load **destroys the Font Maker's restore prompt**, which read as "autosave is broken". Every one was caught by confirming against the real DOM/source before reporting — the third session running that this discipline paid off.

- modal-focus-trap: **first sweep 2026-07-26 (S161, Pass I)** — all **9** `aria-modal="true"` dialogs in the suite, each opened through its genuine opener and tab-cycled in both directions. **7 trapped, 2 not** (both `classroom_dashboard.html`: the first-run setup card and the student-picker overlay). Fixed by extracting the drawer's `_settingsTrapKey` into a shared `_trapTabWithin(container, e)` used by all three dashboard dialogs. Re-verified 12/12 (light+dark × 1280/800, Tab + Shift+Tab) with the settings drawer re-checked for regression after the refactor.

- **S158 (Pass A, 2026-07-25): 5 of 6 ACTIVE classes clean, 1 real hit (fixed)** over `0dc1e50..HEAD` (S148–S157, never A-swept). **Clean:** localStorage-vs-AllTools (0 new localStorage calls at all in the delta), unescaped-input/unsafe-parse (0 new HTML sinks; the check was tightened to also inspect interpolation into *pre-existing* sinks — all 3 new `${…}` sites escape via `esc()`/`CSS.escape`), destructive-bulk (5 new loops, all read-only DOM or derived cache), dead-feature-guard (0 new; S156 removed one of the 4 registered), nameless-controls (0 new; the 4 residual fixed this session → suite at 0). **HIT: pre-ready-i18n, 19 torah_trainer colour-picker `aria-label`s stuck at the raw key, Hebrew-only** — fixed in 790ca80. **The generalisable lesson:** the S152 raw-key detector had only ever been run in a way that surfaces both-language breakage; this instance was **EN-clean and HE-broken**, so every future raw-key scan must run both languages (the sweep is 12 pages × EN/HE = 24 runs). Two detector caveats also recorded in Pattern health (function-parameter false positive; hidden-only nameless false positive). Prior: **S147 (Pass A, 2026-07-24): CLEAN, 0 hits** across the 3 active consequence-critical classes over `43daf88..HEAD` — the surface since S136 (dictionary theme project (Phase 2 Browse-by-Theme UI + POS-aware), trope music-staff (hand-rolled inline SVG via `createElementNS` + validated same-origin `trope_motifs.json`), Torah feature batches (Student handout, per-verse + bulk copy, שם ה׳ detect), #132 solemn banner, S145 dashboard holiday/Shabbat i18n, S146 torah a11y). **unescaped-input:** new innerHTML escaped (dashboard `escapeHtmlDash`, generator answer-key `esc()`/`ctx.title`), 0 new `JSON.parse`, `trope_motifs.json` trusted same-origin fetch + structural validation, trope staff DOM-built (`createElementNS`). **localStorage:** 1 new `setItem` = registered `hebrew{Torah,Trope}Tutor_settings`. **destructive-bulk:** 0 new overwrite loops (bulk-select = `Set` of verse refs). Contract: no `pageerror`, a visible message, localized, not a dead end. **2 of 7 broken, both fixed; 5 fine.** `hebrew_dictionary` **crashed** — a parse-time fetch whose `.catch` ran before the deferred `js/i18n.js`, so a bare `I18n.t()` threw and left the spinner up forever (02f4ed0); `torah_trainer` showed **3 raw i18n key names** on the failure path, the pattern's 3rd instance (9ba18e5). **Exemplar to converge on: `trope_tutor`** — *"Example clips unavailable (couldn't load the trope index) — Learn mode still shows every mark."*: names what broke, what still works, fully localized. **Harness lesson that reshaped the pass:** `sw.js` calls `skipWaiting()`+`clients.claim()`, so the service worker serves `/data/` from `DATA_CACHE` and **bypasses `page.route`** — offline tests need `serviceWorkers: 'block'` or they silently test the happy path. That produced one fabricated finding (Flash Cards) before it was caught; caveat added to CLAUDE.md (2eb5775). Prior: S155 (13th), S154, S144.)

**⚑ Out-of-band (2026-07-28, maintainer-requested pre-merge audit of PR #139; fixes on the same branch; sw.js stays v425; session counter untouched):** before pushing the branch to `main`, the maintainer asked for a session audit. Two passes ran — a targeted fresh-eyes re-verification and an **independent adversarial sweep of the full diff** — and the sweep earned its cost: it found the **"Add Shva" level was a no-op** (subset-only test assertions had let it through green; full entry in Done above) plus the auto-save/50-cap eviction interaction, both fixed after maintainer decisions (bridge-level redesign; new-best-only banking), alongside five smaller hardenings — including one **pre-existing** bug worth naming for other tools: `applySettings` coerced My-Fonts ids to the default font on every settings round trip, so preset/`.ivrit` restores were silently un-choosing uploaded fonts even before the ladder existed. **Reusable lessons, both now demonstrated in this repo's own history: (1) a subset assertion is not an occurrence assertion — verify that promised content *appears*, not just that forbidden content doesn't; (2) an independent adversarial review pass catches what the author's own green suite structurally cannot.** Gates: 2 asked, 2 answered (shva redesign — pedagogy; banking policy — data). The two P2s were caught before any student ever saw the ladder — the branch had never merged.

Two reusable findings came out of the verification, both worth carrying forward: a Playwright assertion over a property the object never had (`card.vowelKeys`) **passes silently and looks like coverage** — a negative control is the cheap defence; and `applyI18n` re-running `showResults` means any *side effect* placed in a render function gets **executed twice on a language switch**, which is the same class of hazard as the CLAUDE.md "applyI18n must be read-only" rule but on the callee's side.

which alone backs **26 call sites** — every key in the AllTools export and `.ivrit` gather. None was a live exploit,
and the ledger entry says so plainly; the point is that the defence in depth had quietly eroded on the one page whose
whole job is importing other people's files.

**Start S169 with the vowel-scheme buttons.** `syncSchemeButtons()` in **all four** scheme tools (generator, flash
cards, dashboard, torah trainer) toggles `.active` and exposes no `aria-pressed`, so a screen-reader user cannot tell
whether Default or TaL AM is live. It is measured, needs **no new copy**, and is one commit. It was left undone in S168
only because the flash-cards and generator slots were spent — and fixing just the two tools that still had slots would
have re-created exactly the shared-code drift S168 had spent an iteration repairing. Then `.weak-tab` ×4 in flash

**The seed queue is empty for the third session running, and gate 1 went unanswered again**, so the fallback skipped
the micro-feature rather than running Pass H. That is the conservative call each time, but it does not converge: until
an H runs there is nothing to offer, so the gate can only ever be answered "skip". **S169 should run H as a second

is still owed its first dedicated C in 168 sessions** — S167 passed it over deliberately and it should win the next C
outright.

**Method notes worth carrying.**
1. **Three harness results were wrong this session, and the standing "verify a clean result as hard as a positive one"
   rule caught all three — but note that two of them were *positives*, not clean results.** A 5-hit localStorage GAP
   list was entirely false (4 were the Font Maker's documented exemption, and `hebrewDashboard_holidayCache` is
   **`sessionStorage`** — the heuristic matched the key name without binding the storage object). A focus-trap sweep
   then reported the dashboard's **projected student picker** leaking focus after one tab; it does not, and the probe
   had latched onto a second visible dialog. **A false positive about a student-facing overlay is exactly as damaging
   as a false clean — extend the rule to say so.**
2. **`offsetParent` is `null` for `position: fixed` elements.** It broke my sweep, and the same idiom sits inside the
   dashboard's own `_trapTabWithin` focusable filter. It does not bite there today (children of a fixed positioned
   ancestor still report an offsetParent), but it is a latent trap for any future fixed-position control inside a
   dialog. Prefer `getBoundingClientRect()` + computed style for visibility.
3. **A negative control can itself be too weak, and a PASSING control is a red flag, not a relief.** The dictionary
   header fix's first control passed, because the sentence template was *already* Hebrew before the fix and only the
   colour name lagged — "contains Hebrew characters" proved nothing. Assert the specific thing the fix changes.
4. **Prefer current-state detection over diff-scoping for properties of the code.** The clone is shallow (55 commits),
   which forced this, and it turned out better: a bare `JSON.parse` or an unregistered storage key is true or false
   about the file as it stands, regardless of when it landed. Keep diffs for *new sink* checks.
5. **When a shared byte-identical block is wrong, check whether one carrier is already right.** The `.ivrit` fix was
   drift repair, not new work — `index.html` had the answer and three carriers had fallen behind it. The negative
   control's shape (12 of 16 failing, with index passing) is the fingerprint of drift.
